[\[161a\]]
[\[161a.1\]]ces bya ba nas snga ma bzhin du rgya cher sbyar te/_rgyal bu stong gi bar du thams cad byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug go/_/khams kyi rgyal po brgyad khri bzhi stong dang /_gzhan yang sems can dung phyur phrag dgu dang /_bye ba phrag gnyis kyang byang chub yang dag 
[\[161a.2\]]par 'dzin du bcug pa dang /_de dag thams cad kyis kyang 'di skad ces smras so/_/bdag cag kyang rang rang gi khyim du mchis la gcig pu dben par mchis nas sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa dag bsam par bgyi'o/_/de skad ces smras nas thams cad rang rang gi khyim dag tu 
[\[161a.3\]]dong ste gcig pu dben par 'dug nas lo bdun gyi bar du sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi yon tan bkod pa'i smon lam sems par byed do/_/rigs kyi bu de nas dus gzhan zhig na bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyi sems la 'di lta bu'i rnam par rtog pa skyes te/_bdag gis srog chags bye ba 
[\[161a.4\]]khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug cing /_bdag gis sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das dge slong gi dge 'dun dpag tu med pa dang bcas pa 'di yang lo bdun gyi bar du yo byad thams cad kyis spyan drangs 
[\[161a.5\]]pas/_gal te bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu bdag gis bsam pa yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur zhing de bzhin du smon lam 'di yang 'grub pa lta na de'i phyir bdag gis lha dang /_lha ma yin dang /_dri za dang /_gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dang /_grul bum rnams 
[\[161a.6\]]mchod sbyin chen po 'di la yang dag par gzud par bya'o snyam mo/_/rigs kyi bu de nas mdun na 'don gyi gtso bo bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul rgyal po rnam thos kyi bu mthong bar 'dod pa dang /_de nas rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu gnod sbyin 'bum phrag du mas yongs su bskor cing mdun du 
[\[161a.7\]]bdar nas/_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul ga la ba der song ste phyin pa dang /_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyi mdun du 'dug nas 'di skad ces smras so/_/bram ze khyod kho bo las ji zhig 'dod/_bram zes smras pa/_khyod su zhig yin/_rnam thos kyi bus smras pa/_bram ze chen po khyod kyis 
[\[161b\]]
[\[161b.1\]]gnod sbyin gyi bdag po rnam thos kyi bu zhes bya ba zhig yod par thos pa de ni kho bo yin na bram ze bsgo ba ci yod/_ci zhig bgyi/_bram zes smras pa/_rgyal po chen po khyod kyang mchod sbyin chen po 'di la spro ba skyed cig_/des smras pa/_bram ze khyod ji ltar 'dod pa de ltar bya'o/_/
[\[161b.2\]]smras pa/_rgyal po chen po de'i phyir khyod kyis kho bo'i tshig gis gnod sbyin rnams bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug la 'di skad ces kyang bsgo shig_/gal te gnod sbyin khyed cag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 'dod pa lta 
[\[161b.3\]]na/_khyed cag deng la rgya mtsho'i pha rol nas tsan dan sa mchog pa dang /_tsan dan sbrul gyi snying po dag long shig_/kha cig gis ni spos sna tshogs dang /_kha cig gis ni me tog sna tshogs dag long shig_/ces bsgo shig dang /_des kho bos nyin gcig bzhin du bcom 
[\[161b.4\]]ldan 'das la mchod pa bya'o/_/rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bu bram ze de ltar bya'o zhes bram ze de'i ltar mnyan nas de nyid du mi snang bar gyur te/_rnga bo che brdungs nas gnod sbyin dang /_srin po dag bsdus te 'di skad ces smras so/_/grogs po dag shes par gyis shig_/gang 
[\[161b.5\]]'dzam bu'i gling 'di na rgyal po rtsibs kyi mu khyud kyi mdun na 'don gyi gtso bo bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul ces bya ba des de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rin po che'i snying po dge slong gi dge 'dun dang bcas pa la lo bdun du yo byad thams cad kyis bsnyen 
[\[161b.6\]]bkur byed kyis/_de'i phyir khyed cag gis de'i dge ba'i rtsa ba la rjes su yi rang bar bya zhing dge ba'i rtsa ba des khyed cag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyed cig_/de'i tshe na gnod sbyin dang /_srin po bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag gis thal mo sbyar te 
[\[161b.7\]]smras pa/_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rin po che'i snying po dge slong gi dge 'dun dpag tu med pa dang bcas pa la lo bdun du yo byad thams cad kyis bsnyen bkur byas pa'i bsod nams kyi rgyun dang /_dge ba'i 
[\[162a\]]
[\[162a.1\]]rgyun gang yin pa'i bsod nams kyi phung po de dag thams cad la bdag cag rjes su yi rang ngo /_/dge ba'i rtsa ba des bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar bya'o/_/rgyal po chen po rnam thos kyi bus smras pa/_khyed cag 
[\[162a.2\]]nyon cig_/khyed cag las gang su dge ba'i rtsa ba 'dod pa dang /_bsod nams 'dod pas lo bdun du gang gi phyir bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi zhal zas sbyor bar 'gyur ba'i tsan dan sa mchog pa dang /_tsan dan sbrul gyi snying 
[\[162a.3\]]po dag rgya mtsho'i pha rol nas long shig_/de nas gnod sbyin dgu khri nyis stong gis tshig 'thun par smras pa/_grogs po dag bdag cag gis lo bdun po 'di la gang gi phyir bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi phyir zhal zas sbyor 
[\[162a.4\]]bar 'gyur ba'i tsan dan sa mchog pa dang /_tsan dan sbrul gyi snying po dag blang bar bya'o/_/gnod sbyin bzhi khri drug stong gis smras pa/_bdag cag gis ni spos blang bar bya'o/_/gnod sbyin lnga khri nyis stong gis smras pa/_bdag cag gis ni me tog sna tshogs dag blang bar bya'o/_/
[\[162a.5\]]gnod sbyin nyi khris smras pa/_bdag cag gis ni bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi phyir sman bcud kyis len sna tshogs dag gi mthu blangs la zhal zas dang /_stsang nan sbyor bar 'gyur ba gang yin pa der mthu gzhug par bya'o/_/gnod sbyin bdun khris smras pa/_grogs 
[\[162a.6\]]po dag bdag cag gis ni bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi zhal zas sbyar bar bya'o/_/rigs kyi bu de nas bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po mthong bar 'dod pa dang /_de nas rgyal po chen po 'phags skyes po bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul ga la ba der 
[\[162a.7\]]song ste phyin cing grul bum bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag du ma dag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa'i bar dang /_de bzhin du rgyal po chen po mig mi bzang dang /_yul 'khor srung dang /_klu dang /_dri za bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag du ma dag 
[\[162b\]]
[\[162b.1\]]gis kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa'i bar du snga ma bzhin no/_/de nas sangs rgyas kyi mthus gling bzhi ba gnyis pa'i 'jig rten skyong ba rnams kyang bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyi gan du 'ongs pa dang /_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis de dag bla na med pa 
[\[162b.2\]]yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug nas/_de dag kyang dong ste rang rang gi 'khor bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug go/_/stong gsum gyi stong chen po'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing gi rnam thos kyi bu ther 'bum 'khor dang 
[\[162b.3\]]bcas pa yang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug go/_/'phags skyes po ther 'bum dang /_mig mi bzang ther 'bum dang /_yul 'khor srung ther 'bum 'khor dang bcas pa dag kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang 
[\[162b.4\]]dag par 'dzin du bcug go/_/rigs kyi bu de nas bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul 'di snyam du sems te/_gal te bdag bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur zhing bdag gi re ba yang yang dag par 'grub par 'gyur la/_gal te bdag gi 
[\[162b.5\]]smon lam yang yang dag par 'grub par 'gyur ba lta na bdag 'dod pa na spyod pa'i lha rnams la mchod sbyin chen po 'di'i bsod nams yang dag par bgo bsha' byed par gyur cig_/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du 'jug par gyur cig_/gal te 
[\[162b.6\]]bdag bsod nams des bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur ba lta na deng lha'i dbang po brgya byin bdag la lta ba'i phyir 'dir 'ong bar gyur cig_/lha'i bu rab 'thab bral dang /_lha'i bu yongs su dga' ldan dang /_lha'i bu rab 
[\[162b.7\]]'phrul dang /_lha'i bu gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyang bdag la blta ba'i phyir 'dir 'ong bar gyur cig snyam mo/_/rigs kyi bu bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis de snyam du sems bskyed ma thag tu lha'i dbang po brgya byin bram ze de lta ba'i phyir 'ongs so/_/lha'i bu rab 'thab bral dang /_
[\[163a\]]
[\[163a.1\]]lha'i bu yongs su dga' ldan dang /_lha'i bu rab 'phrul dang /_lha'i bu gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyang bram ze de lta ba'i phyir 'ongs nas/_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis de dag la khyed cag su yin zhes dris pa dang /_lha'i rgyal po lnga po dag gis rang rang gi ming 
[\[163a.2\]]rus bstan nas 'di skad ces smras so/_/kye bram ze kho bo cag la bsgo ba ni ci zhig yod/_mchod sbyin chen po 'di la kho bo cag gis yo byad dag sbyar bar bya ba lta ci yod/_bram zes smras pa/_bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi phyir khyed cag lha'i 'jig rten na 
[\[163a.3\]]thams cad las khyad par du 'phags pa'i rin po che'i khang pa brtsegs pa dag dang /_rin po che'i shing ljon pa dag dang /_dpag bsam gyi shing ljon pa dag dang /_spos kyi shing ljon pa dag dang /_me tog gi shing ljon pa dag dang /_'bras bu'i shing ljon pa dag dang /_lha'i 
[\[163a.4\]]na bza' dag dang /_lha'i gdan dag dang /_lha'i gding ba dag dang /_lha'i rin po che'i snod dag dang /_lha'i rgyan dag dang /_gdugs dang /_rgyal mtshan dang /_ba dan dang /_lhab lhub dag dang /_lha'i rol mo'i sgra gang dag yod pa de dag thams cad kyis 'dzam bu'i tshal 
[\[163a.5\]]thams cad brgyan par gyis shig_/lha'i rgyal po lnga po de dag gis grogs po de ltar bya'o zhes bram ze'i ltar mnyan te/_bram ze'i drung nas phyir log nas lha'i 'jig rten du dong ste phyin pa dang /_lha'i bu 'khri byed dang /_lha'i bu 'khyil byed dang /_lha'i bu snar ma skyes dang /_
[\[163a.6\]]lha'i bus rtsom dga' rnams bos te 'di skad ces smras so/_/grogs po dag khyed cag deng la 'dzam bu'i gling du babs te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi phyir 'dzam bu'i tshal gyi skyed mos tshal rgyan gyi khyad par sna tshogs dag dang /_gdan dang /_gding ba sna tshogs dag gis ji ltar 
[\[163a.7\]]lha'i 'jig rten 'di brgyan pa de lta bu dag gis brgyan par gyis shig_/grogs po dag rin po che'i khang pa brtsegs pa 'di lta bu yang brtsig tu chug shig_/'di lta ste/_khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che'i ba gam can 'di ci 'dra ba de lta bu'i rin po che'i khang pa brtsegs brtsig tu 
[\[163b\]]
[\[163b.1\]]chug shig_/lha'i bu de lngas lha'i rgyal po rnams kyi ltar mnyan nas 'dzam bu'i gling du babs te/_bcom ldan 'das kyi phyir mtshan gcig gis 'dzam bu'i tshal gyi skyed mos tshal thams cad rin po che'i shing ljon pa dag dang /_rgyal mtshan gyi bar dag gi rgyan de lta bu dag gis brgyan 
[\[163b.2\]]to/_/'di lta ste/_dper na lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyi khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che'i ba gam can ci 'dra ba de lta bu'i khang pa brtsegs pa yang brtsigs te/_'dzam bu'i tshal thams cad lha'i rgyan gyis rnam pa thams cad du yongs su mthar thug par brgyan nas lha'i rgyal po rnams kyi 
[\[163b.3\]]gan du song ste smras pa/_grogs po dag mkhyen par mdzod cig_/ji ltar lha'i 'jig rten 'di legs par brgyan pa de bzhin du/_bcom ldan 'das kyi phyir 'dzam bu'i tshal gyi skyed mos tshal yang lha'i rgyan gyi khyad par rnams kyis rnam pa thams cad du yongs su mthar thug par 
[\[163b.4\]]brgyan cing 'di lta ste/_dper na lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyi khang pa brtsegs pa rin po che'i ba gam can ci 'dra ba de lta bu'i rin po che thams cad kyi khang pa brtsegs pa yang brtsegs lags te/_grogs po dag lha'i 'jig rten dang /_'dzam bu'i gling gi 'dzam bu'i tshal gyi skyed 
[\[163b.5\]]mos tshal ni khyad par cung zad kyang ma mchis so/_/de nas lha'i rgyal po lnga po brgya byin dang rab 'thab bral dang /_yongs su dga' ldan dang /_rab 'phrul dang /_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed de dag 'dzam bu'i gling du babs nas bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyi drung du song ste 'di skad 
[\[163b.6\]]ces smras so/_/bram ze bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi phyir 'dzam bu'i tshal ni brgyan zin na yang kho bo cag gis bya ba lta ci yod/_de skad ces smras pa dang /_bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis lha'i bu lnga po de dag la 'di skad ces smras so/_/lha'i rgyal 
[\[163b.7\]]po khyed lnga ni lha'i ris so so na rgyal srid byed pa yin pas de na khyed cag gi dbang du 'gyur gyis lha'i rgyal po dag deng la rang rang gi lha'i 'khor rnams bsdus te bcom ldan 'das dang dge slong gi dge 'dun la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba'i phyir 'dzam bu'i gling du sheg 
[\[164a\]]
[\[164a.1\]]la bcom ldan 'das las chos kyang nyon cig_/de nas lha'i rgyal po de lnga rang rang gi gnas kyi khyad par rnams su dong ste phyin pa dang /_lha'i dbang po brgya byin gyis sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha rnams bsdus te 'di skad ces smras so/_/grogs po dag shes par 
[\[164a.2\]]gyis shig_/'dzam bu'i gling na rgyal po rtsibs kyi mu khyud kyi mdun na 'don gyi gtso bo bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul ces bya ba des de bzhin gshegs pa rin po che'i snying po dge slong gi dge 'dun dpag tu med pa dang bcas pa la lo bdun du yo byad thams cad kyis spyan drangs shing ri mor byed de/_bram 
[\[164a.3\]]ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyis yang dag par 'dzin du bcug pas kho bo cag gis kyang bcom ldan 'das dang dge slong gi dge 'dun gyi phyir skyed mos tshal thams cad brgyan zin gyis/_de'i phyir khyod kyis kyang dge ba'i rtsa ba de la rjes su yi rang bar gyis la/_bla na med pa yang dag par 
[\[164a.4\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems skyed cig_/de'i tshe na sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag gis thal mo sbyar te tshig tu smras pa/_grogs po bdag cag gis bsod nams kyi phung po de la rjes su yi rang bar bya zhing rjes su yi rang ba de las byung ba'i bsod 
[\[164a.5\]]nams gang yin pa de dag thams cad kyang bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su bsngo bar bya'o/_/lha'i bu rab 'thab bral gyis kyang 'thab bral gyi lha'i bu rnams bsdus te/_rgya cher snga ma bzhin du sbyar bar bya'o/_/lha'i bu yongs su dga' ldan dang /_lha'i 
[\[164a.6\]]bu 'phrul dga' dang /_lha'i bu gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyis kyang gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi bar gyi lha'i bu dag bsdus nas lha'i bu bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag gis thal mo sbyar te tshig tu smras pa/_grogs po dag bdag cag gis dge ba'i rtsa ba rnams kyi bsod nams 
[\[164a.7\]]gang yin pa de la rjes su yi rang bar bya zhing /_de dag thams cad bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu yongs su bsngo bar byas/_grogs po dag de'i phyir 'dzam bu'i gling du 'bab cing bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_
[\[164b\]]
[\[164b.1\]]bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir 'dong ngo /_/lha'i rgyal po de lnga nub mo lha'i rgyal po re re zhing yang skyes pa dang /_bud med dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag dang lhan cig tu 'dzam bu'i gling du babs te/_bcom ldan 'das dang dge 
[\[164b.2\]]slong gi dge 'dun gyi zhabs la mgo bos phyag 'tshal te/_bcom ldan 'das la chos nyan to/_/nam mkha'i dkyil na gnas pa'i lha rnams kyang bcom ldan 'das la lha'i me tog ud pa la dang /_pad ma dang /_ku mu da dang /_pad ma dkar po dang /_sna ma dang /_bar shi ka dang /_a 
[\[164b.3\]]ti mug ta ka dang /_tsam pa ka dang /_man dA ra ba dang /_man dA ra ba chen po'i me tog gi char dag 'bebs shing lha'i rol mo'i sgra dag kyang rab tu sgrogs par byed do/_/rigs kyi bu gzhan yang bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gyi sems la sems kyi yongs su rtog pa 'di lta bu skyes te/_gal 
[\[164b.4\]]te bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub la bdag gi re ba yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur zhing smon lam 'di yang 'grub pa lta na/_'di ltar lha ma yin dag kyang byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du 'jug par gyur cig snyam mo/_/rigs kyi bu des sems bskyed ma thag tu gnod sbyin 
[\[164b.5\]]gyi dbang po lnga bram ze de ga la ba der dong ste lhags pa nas/_bram ze'i tshig gis lha ma yin skyes pa dang /_bud med dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo dang bcas pa bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed cing /_bcom 
[\[164b.6\]]ldan 'das kyi drung du 'ongs nas chos nyan pa'i bar du snga ma bzhin du rgya cher sbyar ro/_/de bzhin du bram ze de bdud 'dod pa dang /_de'i tshe na bdud gang po zhes bya ba 'ongs pa nas bdud kyi ris kyi lha'i bu skyes pa dang /_bud med dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo dang bcas pa bye ba khrag 
[\[164b.7\]]khrig 'bum phrag du ma dag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed pa'i bar dang /_chos mnyan pa'i phyir lhags pa'i bar du snga ma bzhin du sbyar ro/_/rigs kyi bu de nas yang bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul tshangs pa chen po gnas pa'i grong khyer zhes bya ba 'ong 
[\[165a\]]
[\[165a.1\]]bar 'dod pa nas bram ze las mnyan cing tshangs pa'i 'jig rten du srog chags bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong mang po dag gis bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed nas bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun la blta ba dang /_phyag 
[\[165a.2\]]bya ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_bcom ldan 'das las chos mnyan pa'i phyir de nas babs pa'i bar du snga ma bzhin du sbyar ro/_/rigs kyi bu de nas yang bram ze rgya mtsho'i rdul gling bzhi pa gnyis pa'i brgya byin mthong bar 'dod cing lha'i bu rab 'thab bral dang /_yongs 
[\[165a.3\]]su dga' ldan dang /_rab 'phrul dang /_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed rnams dang mthong bar 'dod pa dang /_bcom ldan 'das kyi mthus lha'i rgyal po de lnga yang bram ze'i drung du lhags nas bram ze des de dag yang dag par rjes su bstan pa dang /_de dag kyang rang rang gi lha'i gnas dag tu 
[\[165a.4\]]dong nas bram ze'i tshig gis rang rang gi 'khor rnams kyang yang dag par 'dzin du bcug go/_/de bzhin du sum cu rtsa gsum pa'i lha'i bu skyes pa dang /_bud med dang /_khye'u dang /_bu mo dang bcas pa bye ba khrag khrig 'bum phrag mang po dag gis kyang bla na med pa yang dag par 
[\[165a.5\]]rdzogs pa'i byang chub tu sems bskyed nas de dag kyang brgya byin dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 'das dang /_dge slong gi dge 'dun la blta ba dang /_phyag bya ba dang /_bsnyen bkur bya ba dang /_bcom ldan 'das las chos mnyan pa'i phyir gling bzhi pa 'dir lhags so/_/de bzhin du 
[\[165a.6\]]lha'i bu rab 'thab bral dang /_yongs su dga' ldan dang /_'phrul dga' dang /_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed rnams kyis kyang gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi bar gyi lha'i bu rnams byang chub yang dag par 'dzin du bcug nas/_gzhan 'phrul dbang byed kyi bar gyi lha'i bu skyes pa dang /_

[\[184b.1\]]pa'i byang chub tu re ba yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba lta na/_de'i slad du pad ma 'di dag nam mkha' la gnas par gyur la/_de ltar nam mkha' la gnas nas char bzhin du yang bab par gyur cig_/bcom ldan 'das kyis bka' stsal pa/_rigs kyi bu nam mkha' 
[\[184b.2\]]la pad ma dag gis shin tu myur bar rgyas btab pas na de'i phyir rigs kyi bu khyod kyang nam mkha'i phyag rgya zhes bya bar gyur cig_/nam mkha'i phyag rgya khyod ni ma 'ongs pa'i dus na bskal pa grangs med pa gang gA'i klung gcig gi bye ma snyed 'das nas grangs med pa gnyis pa 
[\[184b.3\]]la bab pa na shar lho'i phyogs logs su sangs rgyas kyi zhing gang gA'i klung bye ba phrag 'bum gyi bye ma snyed 'das pa na 'jig rten gyi khams pad ma zhes bya ba zhig yod de/_khyod der byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom

[\[185a.1\]]lta bu yongs su rdzogs par 'gyur ba lta na/_de'i slad du phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed kyi nam mkha' thams cad las rin po che sna bdun gyi gdugs gser gyi dra ba dag gis g.yogs pa/_rin po che sna bdun gyi

[\[185b.1\]]rgya chen po btab ste/_khyod kyi bsod nams kyi rgyu 'thun pa des phyogs bcu'i 'jig rten gyi khams gang gA'i klung gi bye ma snyed mang po dag tshad gcig tu gyur la yid du 'ong ba'i sgra 'bum phrag mang po dag gis bskul ba'i sangs rgyas kyi zhing der rigs kyi bu khyod rnam par snang byed

[\[208a.3\]]dang /_gti mug gi sems cha mnyam pa rnams dang /_dge ba dang mi dge ba'i bsam pa la mi gnas pa'i skye dgu tshe lo bzhi khri thub pa na bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar gyur cig_/de bzhin gshegs pa rin po che'i 
[\[208a.4\]]snying pos bka' stsal pa/_bskal pa grangs med pa gang gA'i klung gcig gi bye ma snyed 'das nas bskal pa grangs med gnyis pa la bab pa na 'jig rten gyi khams 'di ni mi mjed ces bya bar 'gyur te/_ci'i phyir 'jig rten gyi khams de ni mi mjed ces bya zhe na/_sems 
[\[208a.5\]]can de dag 'dod chags la mi mjed pa dang /_de dag zhe sdang la mi mjed pa dang /_gti mug la mi mjed pa dang /_nyon mongs pa'i 'ching ba rnams la mi mjed pas de'i phyir 'jig rten gyi khams de ni mi mjed ces bya'o/_/'jig rten gyi khams mi mjed der bskal pa chen po 
[\[208a.6\]]bzang po zhes bya ba 'byung bar 'gyur te/_ci'i phyir bzang po zhes bya zhe na/_bskal pa chen po bzang po de la sems can 'dod chags dang /_zhe sdang dang /_gti mug spyod pa rnams kyi nang du rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das thugs rje chen po dang ldan pa stong 'byung 
[\[208a.7\]]bar 'gyur ba'i phyir te/_skyes bu dam pa khyod kyang bskal pa chen po bzang po byung zhing /_skye dgu lo bzhi khri thub pa na thams cad kyi dang por bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub mngon par rdzogs par 'tshang rgya bar 'gyur te/_de bzhin gshegs pa 'khor ba

[\[208b.1\]]'jig ces bya ba sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das kyi bar du 'gyur zhing /_theg pa gsum po dag gis chos ston par 'gyur te/_'khor ba'i chu bos bdas pa'i sems can bgrang ba las 'das pa dag kyang sgrol bar 'gyur zhing mya ngan las 'das pa'i pha rol tu 'god

[\[209a.1\]]blo rab blo gros ldan/_/drang srong dam pa khyod mdzes thub gtso khyu mchog lags/_/yon tan brgyas bltam yon tan mang po stsol ba khyod/_/thub mchog bde ba mdzad pa mi yi dam pas btud/_/srid pa gsum na khyod 'dra'i sems can gzhan ma

[\[260a.4\]]ldan 'das rigs kyi bu'am/_rigs kyi bu mo tshogs ji lta bu dang ldan na bstan par gnas pa lags/_ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo bstan pa ji lta bus ni brgyan pa lags/_rigs kyi bu de nas de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rin po 
[\[260a.5\]]che'i snying pos byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po snying rje chen po dang ldan pa de la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to/_/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa khyod kyi zhu ba bzang po spobs pa dge ste/_snying rje chen po dang ldan pa khyod de ltar de bzhin gshegs pa la don de lta bu yongs 
[\[260a.6\]]su zhu bar sems pa ni snying rje chen po dang ldan pa khyod sems can dpag tu med grangs med pa rnams la phan pa byed pa dang /_gces spras byed pa rgya chen po skyes pa yin te/_legs so legs so/_/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa de'i phyir legs par rab tu nyon la yid la 
[\[260a.7\]]zung shig dang ngas khyod la bshad par bya'o/_/snying rje chen po dang ldan pa byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin dpa' bar 'gro ba zhes bya ba yod do/_/byang chub sems dpa'i ting nge 'dzin de la gnas pa ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad 
[\[260b\]]
[\[260b.1\]]la rjes su zhugs pa yin no/_/rin chen phyag rgya zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin yod de/_des ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la phyag rgyas 'debs par byed do/_/seng ge rnam par rtse ba zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin yod de/_de la gnas nas ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyis rnam par 
[\[260b.2\]]rtse bar byed do/_/zla ba bzang po zhes bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad snang bar byed do/_/zla ba'i rgyal mtshan tog ces bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi rgyal mtshan 'dzin par byed do/_/chos thams cad las mngon par 'phags pa'i 
[\[260b.3\]]ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad khong du chud par byed do/_/phyag rgya rnam par lta ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi spyi gtsug rnam par lta bar byed do/_/chos kyi dbyings su rnam par song ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni byang chub sems dpa' chos 
[\[260b.4\]]kyi dbyings thams cad rnam par nges par 'gro bar byed do/_/nges pa'i rgyal mtshan tog gi ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi rgyal mtshan 'dzin par byed do/_/rdo rje'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad mi phyed par byed do/_/chos la 'jug pa'i 
[\[260b.5\]]phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la phyag rgyas 'debs par byed do/_/ting nge 'dzin gyi rgyal po shin tu brtan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rgyal po nyid du rab tu gnas par byed do/_/'od zer 'gyed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin 
[\[260b.6\]]thams cad la 'od zer 'gyed par byed do/_/stobs kyi brtson 'grus kyi ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la stobs dang brtson 'grus su byed do/_/yang dag par 'phags pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad las gyen du 'phags par byed do/_/nges pa'i tshig 
[\[260b.7\]]ston pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi tshig la 'jug par byed do/_/tshig [{]bla dgas [,]bla dwags [}]la 'jug pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi ming gi rjes su 'jug par byed do/_/phyogs la rnam par lta ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams 
[\[261a\]]
[\[261a.1\]]cad la rnam par lta bar byed do/_/chos thams cad rab tu 'byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad kyis rab tu dbye ba rjes su rtogs par byed do/_/phyag rgya yongs su 'dzin pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi phyag rgya 'dzin par byed 
[\[261a.2\]]do/_/chos thams cad la dben pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad las dben pa'i chos nyid la rjes su 'jug par byed do/_/brjed pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad mi brjed par byed do/_/chos thams cad las mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 
[\[261a.3\]]'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la mi g.yo ba nyid du gnas par byed do/_/chos thams cad yang dag par 'du ba rgya mtsho'i phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad sdud cing yang dag par 'du bar byed do/_/chos thams cad la rlom sems med pa'i ting 
[\[261a.4\]]nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la 'byung ba dang /_'jug pa'i rlom sems med par byed do/_/nam mkha' khyab par byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la nam mkha' ltar khyab par byed do/_/chos thams cad rgyun mi gcod pa'i ting nge 
[\[261a.5\]]'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad rgyun mi 'chad par byed do/_/rdo rje'i dkyil 'khor gyi ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi dkyil 'khor 'dzin par byed do/_/chos thams cad ro gcig pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi bcud 
[\[261a.6\]]'dzin par byed do/_/rin chen rgyal ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni yo byad kyi nyon mongs pa thams cad spong bar byed do/_/chos thams cad skye ba med pa dang /_pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad skye ba med pa dang /_'gag pa med par ston par byed do/_/rnam par snang ba'i ting nge 'dzin 
[\[261a.7\]]gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad lam me lhan ne lhang nger byed do/_/chos thams cad 'gag pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad rnam par 'byed par byed do/_/tshol ba med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la ting nge 'dzin gyi chos 
[\[261b\]]
[\[261b.1\]]rnams nam yang tshol bar mi byed do/_/gnas la rten pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la gnas pa'i chos nam yang yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/nam mkha' lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad nam mkha'i ngo 
[\[261b.2\]]bo nyid lta bur snying po med pa nyid du yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/sems med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la sems dang sems las byung ba'i chos rnams spong bar byed do/_/gzugs mu med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni gzugs kun tu snang 
[\[261b.3\]]bar byed do/_/dri ma med pa'i sgron ma'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la sgron ma bzhin du byed do/_/chos thams cad mu med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la ye shes mu med par ston par byed do/_/glog lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 
[\[261b.4\]]'dzin thams cad la shes pa mu med pa ston par byed do/_/'od thams cad byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad 'od byed pa'i sgo nye bar ston par byed do/_/khams kyi mtha'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin mtha' med pa thams cad rnam par shes par bya 
[\[261b.5\]]ba'i phyir nye bar ston par byed do/_/ting nge 'dzin dag pa dam pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin gyi chos rnams las stong pa nyid rjes su thob par byed do/_/lhun po ltar bkra ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad gsog nyid du kun tu ston par byed do/_/'od dri ma med 
[\[261b.6\]]pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi dri ma sel bar byed do/_/chos thams cad kyi brda'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad ma 'dres pa nyid du yang dag par ston par byed do/_/dga' ba byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la dga' 
[\[261b.7\]]ba rab tu thob par byed do/_/chos thams cad kyi ngo bo nyid la rnam par rtse ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la ngo bo nyid mi dmigs pa ston par byed do/_/glog 'gyur ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la mi zad pa nyid ston par byed do/_/chos 
[\[262a\]]
[\[262a.1\]]thams cad la rnam par g.yeng ba med cing rdul dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi shes pa rdul dang bral ba nye bar ston par byed do/_/mi zad pa dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi zad pa med pa dang /_zad 
[\[262a.2\]]pa med pa ma yin pa yang dag par ston par byed do/_/chos thams cad bsam gyis mi khyab cing rnam par dag pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad mig yor lta bur ston par byed do/_/gzi brjid yod pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la ye shes 
[\[262a.3\]]'bar bar byed do/_/zad pa dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad zad pa med pa dang /_nyams pa med par ston par byed do/_/mi g.yo ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la g.yo bar mi byed/_'gul bar mi byed/_'phro bar mi byed do/_/
[\[262a.4\]]rnam par 'phel ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin dang snyoms par 'jug pa thams cad la shes bya rnam par 'phel bar yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/nyi ma'i sgron ma'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la 'od zer gyi sgo dag 'gyed par byed do/_/zla ba 
[\[262a.5\]]dri ma med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la snang bar byed do/_/snang ba dag pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la so so yang dag par rig pa bzhi rab tu thob par byed do/_/bya ba dang mi bya ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni bya ba dang mi bya ba'i bya ba byed cing 
[\[262a.6\]]ye shes rtogs kyang yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/rdo rje lta bu'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad nges par 'byed par byed cing gang la 'byed pa yang yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/sems gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni sems g.yo bar mi 
[\[262a.7\]]byed/_'gul bar mi byed/_snang bar mi byed cing gnod par 'gyur ba yang med la/_de 'di snyam du 'di ni sems yin no snyam du yang mi sems par byed do/_/kun tu snang ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la snang ba yang dag par rjes su mthong bar 
[\[262b\]]
[\[262b.1\]]byed do/_/rab tu gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rab tu gnas pa nyid kyis rab tu gnas par byed do/_/rin chen brtsegs pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rin po che brtsegs pa lta bur kun tu snang bar byed do/_/chos dam pa'i 
[\[262b.2\]]phyag rgya'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la phyag rgya 'debs par byed cing chos thams cad mnyam pa nyid kyi phyir mnyam pa nyid ma gtogs pa'i chos 'ga' yang yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/dga' ba spong ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad 
[\[262b.3\]]la dga' ba spong bar byed do/_/chos kyi sgron ma'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la yi ge mi mnyam pa nyid rab tu 'thob par byed do/_/yi ge dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la yi ge gcig kyang mi dmigs par byed do/_/dmigs pa gcod 
[\[262b.4\]]pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la dmigs pa rnam par gcod par byed do/_/'gyur ba med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la 'gyur ba mi dmigs par byed do/_/rang bzhin rnam par dag pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la phan 'dogs 
[\[262b.5\]]pa mi dmigs par byed do/_/gnas med par rgyu ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la gnas mi dmigs par byed do/_/rab rib dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad kyi spyod pa yang dag par rjes su mi mthong zhing mun pa'i yul las 
[\[262b.6\]]yang dag par 'da' bar byed do/_/yon tan thams cad kyi tshogs su gyur pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la bstsags pa spong bar byed do/_/sems med par gnas pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la sems zhes bya ba mi dmigs par byed do/_/
[\[262b.7\]]byang chub kyi yan lag yod pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad rtogs par byed do/_/dran pa rnam par 'phro ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la spobs pa grangs med pa thob par byed do/_/de byed pa'i shes pa rnam par dag pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams 
[\[263a\]]
[\[263a.1\]]cad la mi mnyam pa dang mnyam pa nyid rab tu thob par byed do/_/ye shes tog gi ting nge 'dzin gyis ni khams gsum thams cad las yang dag par 'da' bar byed do/_/shes pa nye bar gcod pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad rnam par gcod 
[\[263a.2\]]pa yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/ye shes rnam par 'phro ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad kyi rnam par 'phro ba nyid rjes su thob par byed do/_/gnas med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad gnas su gyur pa med pa nyid du yang dag par rjes su 
[\[263a.3\]]mthong bar byed do/_/bkod pa gcig pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos 'ga' yang gnyis su yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/rnam par sgrub pa dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad kyi rnam par bsgrub par yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/
[\[263a.4\]]srid pa'i gzhi thams cad rnam par sel ba thams cad du gtogs pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la nges par 'byed pa'i ye shes 'jug cing gang la 'jug pa 'ga' yang mi rnyed par byed do/_/brda dang sgra la 'jug pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni sgra dang brda thams cad 
[\[263a.5\]]kyi rjes su 'jug par byed do/_/sgra dbyangs kyi yi ge dang bral ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad yi ge dang bral bar yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/ye shes kyi sgron ma dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la gzi brjid kyis lam me 
[\[263a.6\]]lhan ne lhang nger byed do/_/ye shes mchog gi mtshan nyid rnam par bsgyings pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la yongs su dag pa'i mtshan nyid ston par byed do/_/mngon par shes pa med pa'i mtshan nyid dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la 
[\[263a.7\]]mngon par rig pa med par yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos dang ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rnam pa thams cad kyi mchog dang ldan par byed do/_/sdug bsngal thams cad shin tu spong 
[\[263b\]]
[\[263b.1\]]ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la gnas pa med pa yang dag par rjes su mthong bar byed do/_/byed rgyu mi zad pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la zad par yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/gzungs kyi gzhi'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams 
[\[263b.2\]]cad dang /_chos thams cad 'dzin cing yang dag pa nyid dang /_log pa nyid yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/'gag pa dang 'gal ba rab tu zhi ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad dang 'thun pa dang 'gal ba yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/
[\[263b.3\]]'od dri ma med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la 'dus byas kyi dri ma dang bral ba yang dag par rjes su mi mthong bar byed do/_/snying po dang ldan pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la snying po med pa mi dmigs par byed do/_/zla ba nya ba dri ma med pa'i 
[\[263b.4\]]ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la yon tan yongs su rdzogs par byed do/_/bkod pa chen po'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la bkod pa chen po dang ldan par byed do/_/snang ba thams cad rab tu 'byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni/_chos thams 
[\[263b.5\]]cad la ye shes kyis snang bar byed do/_/mnyam pa nyid rnam par snang bar byed pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la rtse gcig pa nyid rab tu thob par byed do/_/nyon mongs pa med pa dang nyon mongs pa dang bcas pa thams cad yang dag par 'du ba'i ting nge 
[\[263b.6\]]'dzin gyis ni ting nge 'dzin thams cad la nyon mongs pa med par byed do/_/rlung gi gnas kyi ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la gnas par mi byed do/_/de bzhin nyid la gnas shing sems med pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad kyi de bzhin nyid las phyir mi ldog par byed 
[\[263b.7\]]do/_/lus kyi skyon yang dag par sel ba'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni chos thams cad la 'jig tshogs mi dmigs par byed do/_/dag gi skyon rnam par 'jig pas nam mkha' ltar gyur pa'i ting nge 'dzin gyis ni byang chub sems dpa' chos thams cad la ngag gi las mi dmigs par byed 
[\[264a\]]
[\[264a.1\]]do/_/nam mkha' ltar chags pa med la rnam par grol zhing gos pa med pa'i ting nge 'dzin la gnas pa'i byang chub sems dpa' ni chos thams cad la nam mkha' ltar chags pa med pa nyid rjes su thob par 'gyur te/_de ni theg pa chen po la yang dag par zhugs 
[\[264a.2\]]pa'i byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi ting nge 'dzin gyi sgo bstan pa yin no/_/de la byang chub sems dpa' sems dpa' chen po rnams kyi tshogs rnam par dag pa'i sgo sdud pa'i chos kyi rnam grangs gang zhe na/_byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi sbyin pa'i tshogs ni 
[\[264a.3\]]sems can thams cad yongs su smin par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi tshul khrims kyi tshogs ni smon lam yongs su rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bzod pa'i tshogs ni mtshan dang dpe byad bzang po yongs su 
[\[264a.4\]]rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi brtson 'grus kyi tshogs ni dgos pa thams cad yongs su rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bsam gtan gyi tshogs ni cang shes kyi sems su byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub 
[\[264a.5\]]sems dpa' rnams kyi shes rab kyi tshogs ni nyon mongs pa thams cad yongs su shes par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi thos pa'i tshogs ni spobs pa thogs pa med par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi bsod nams kyi tshogs 
[\[264a.6\]]ni sems can thams cad nye bar 'tsho bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi ye shes kyi tshogs ni shes pa thogs pa med par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi zhi gnas kyi tshogs ni sems las su rung bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/
[\[264a.7\]]byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi lhag mthong gi tshogs ni the tsom dang bral bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi byams pa'i tshogs ni zhe 'gras pa'i sems med par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi snying rje'i tshogs ni sems 
[\[264b\]]
[\[264b.1\]]can yongs su smin par bya ba la skyo ba med par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dga' ba'i tshogs ni chos kyi kun dga' la dga' zhing dga' bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi btang snyoms kyi tshogs ni rjes su chags pa dang /_khong 
[\[264b.2\]]khro ba spong bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi chos nyan pa'i tshogs ni sgrib pa spong bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi mngon par 'byung ba'i tshogs ni yongs su 'dzin pa thams cad spong bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems 
[\[264b.3\]]dpa' rnams kyi dgon par gnas pa'i tshogs ni dge ba'i rtsa ba'i las byas pa chud mi za bar byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dran pa'i tshogs ni gzungs thob par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi blo gros kyi tshogs ni blo rab 
[\[264b.4\]]tu 'byed par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rtogs pa'i tshogs ni don gyi rnam pa rjes su rtogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dran pa nye bar gzhag pa'i tshogs ni/_lus dang /_tshor ba dang /_sems dang /_chos rjes 
[\[264b.5\]]su rtogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi yang dag par spong ba'i tshogs ni mi dge ba'i chos thams cad spong ba dang dge ba'i chos thams cad sgom par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi rdzu 'phrul gyi rkang pa'i tshogs ni lus 
[\[264b.6\]]dang sems yang ba nyid du byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi dbang po'i tshogs ni sems can thams cad kyi dbang po yongs su rdzogs par byed par 'gyur ro/_/byang chub sems dpa' rnams kyi stobs kyi tshogs ni nyon mongs pa thams cad 'joms par

[\[265a\]]
